Meridian 挑战集：单路、候选并集与 RRF

待人工复核 · PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW。当前指标仅基于冻结的初始标注，不是已确认的项目成果。合成诊断集，不代表真实流量或统计显著性。

96份合成资料、105个实际切片、36题（30道有答案、6道无答案）。282次本地非空 embedding、72次本地关键词搜索，云端调用0。

原模型与 BGE-M3 均只编码正文；关键词分别搜正文和正文＋文件名。RRF 等权、常数60，每路50，截断30，评价前5。不调权重、不删除失败题、不修改旧结果。

先复核标注 · 结果分析 · 检索前冻结协议 · 完整指标与排名

本轮观察（标注待复核）

新集不再是 BGE-M3 Recall@5 满分，但没有出现总体融合获胜：BGE-M3 单路 Recall@5 为98.33%、nDCG@5为0.9017；两种 RRF 均为86.67%、0.8002。原模型 Recall@5 融合前后均为86.67%，nDCG 从0.7677升至0.7967。

在每路前25和前50两个深度，关键词独有的初始相关切片数均为0；并集没有扩大对应向量单路的相关证据集合。这里的收益主要可能来自重排，而不是补回向量候选中完全不存在的证据。

c28 是互补排序收益：两份证据从 BGE-M3 第8、4名变为RRF第5、1名，前五事实齐全率从5/6提高到6/6。但 c08、c11、c12、c24 的跨语言证据被挤出前五；c03 与 c15 则把相似但适用范围不对的切片排到了正确证据之前。

仍只有105个切片，每路取50已覆盖接近半库，候选召回仍接近天花板。不能把这次合成挑战集称为大规模真实流量基准，也不能据此断言混合检索普遍无效。

  1. d07--c000 · 分级 0
    # Edge 中国区测试环境 / E-218
测试环境 E-218 的处理允许清空 spool 并重新生成模拟数据。本操作只适用于没有真实客户数据的隔离测试机，生产采集器不得照搬。
  2. d05--c000 · 分级 3
    # Edge 中国区生产环境 / E-218
边缘采集器出现 E-218 表示离线队列磁盘已满。先导出未发送记录，再清理已确认送达的批次。禁止直接删除整个 spool 目录；这样会丢失尚未送达的记录。处理后检查队列水位。
  3. d06--c000 · 分级 0
    # Edge 中国区生产环境 / E-281
E-281 表示采集器时钟偏差超过五分钟。同步 NTP，确认时区和宿主机时间后重试。此错误与离线队列容量无关，不应清理 spool。
  4. d46--c000 · 分级 0
    # 中国区测试 API
隔离测试环境的批量导出上限为每次 100000 行，用于验证大文件流程。测试额度不代表生产环境团队版或企业版的额度。
  5. d45--c000 · 分级 0
    # 中国区生产 API：企业版

  5. d20--c000 · 分级 0

    # RX-802 mirrored storage

  2. d11--c000 · 分级 0
    # RX-820 flashing amber
A flashing amber mirror LED means synchronization is active. Wait for synchronization to finish before maintenance. Do not replace the battery on this signal alone; steady amber M12 is a separate fault.
  3. d09--c000 · 分级 3
    # RX-820 mirrored storage
For the RX-820 controller, a steady amber mirror LED with code M12 requires replacing the standby cache battery. Keep the active controller online. Flashing amber has a different meaning and is not covered by this procedure.

问题、证据和完整标注复核
KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d05--c000 · 分级 0
    # Edge 中国区生产环境 / E-218
边缘采集器出现 E-218 表示离线队列磁盘已满。先导出未发送记录，再清理已确认送达的批次。禁止直接删除整个 spool 目录；这样会丢失尚未送达的记录。处理后检查队列水位。
  2. d13--c000 · 分级 3
    # PAY_SETTLE_V2 consumer
When moving PAY_SETTLE_V2 to another consumer group, commit the final source offset before disabling the old group. Start the new group from that recorded offset. This queue carries settlement events; replaying from earliest can duplicate external settlement requests.
  3. d32--c000 · 分级 0
    # 消费者延迟与扩容
工作进程 CPU 很低但待处理消息持续增加时，先检查消费是否被下游限流阻塞。查看 retry-after 和下游并发配额，再决定是否扩容。盲目增加消费者可能加重限流，低 CPU 不等于队列没有工作。
  4. d14--c000 · 分级 0
    # PAY_SETTLE_V1 consumer
PAY_SETTLE_V1 is the retired sandbox demonstration queue. A fresh consumer may start from earliest because events go to a disposable ledger. The instructions are not valid for PAY_SETTLE_V2 or any live settlement connector.
  5. d87--c001 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：排空
暂停接收后，等待 in_flight_settlements 降到0再停止消费者。队列长度为0并不证明外部银行提交已结束。

PAY_STATUS_V2 carries read-model notifications. Rebuilding its materialized status view from earliest is supported. These events do not submit settlements to a bank. Similar queue names do not imply identical replay safety.
  5. d87--c001 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：排空
暂停接收后，等待 in_flight_settlements 降到0再停止消费者。队列长度为0并不证明外部银行提交已结束。
BGE_M3_BODY_RRF · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d13--c000 · 分级 3
    # PAY_SETTLE_V2 consumer
When moving PAY_SETTLE_V2 to another consumer group, commit the final source offset before disabling the old group. Start the new group from that recorded offset. This queue carries settlement events; replaying from earliest can duplicate external settlement requests.
  2. d14--c000 · 分级 0
    # PAY_SETTLE_V1 consumer
PAY_SETTLE_V1 is the retired sandbox demonstration queue. A fresh consumer may start from earliest because events go to a disposable ledger. The instructions are not valid for PAY_SETTLE_V2 or any live settlement connector.
  3. d87--c001 · 分级 0

  4. d32--c000 · 分级 0
    # 消费者延迟与扩容
工作进程 CPU 很低但待处理消息持续增加时，先检查消费是否被下游限流阻塞。查看 retry-after 和下游并发配额，再决定是否扩容。盲目增加消费者可能加重限流，低 CPU 不等于队列没有工作。
  5. d87--c000 · 分级 0

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d17--c000 · 分级 3
    # pool.acquireTimeoutMs
数据库连接池参数 pool.acquireTimeoutMs 控制调用方等待空闲连接的最长时间，单位为毫秒。它不终止已经执行的 SQL。等待超时应检查连接泄漏和并发量，而不是直接增加语句超时。
  2. d18--c000 · 分级 0
    # pool.statementTimeoutMs
pool.statementTimeoutMs 控制 SQL 执行时间，单位为毫秒；超过限制会请求取消当前语句。它不控制从连接池取得连接之前的排队等待。
  3. d38--c000 · 分级 0
    # 工作区日志滚动
应用重启后历史日志看起来消失，但持久卷还在时，先检查日志轮转后的归档文件和查询时间范围。新的活动日志文件从空白开始不代表归档被删除。不要立即把持久卷重新初始化。
  4. d19--c000 · 分级 0
    # pool.idleTimeoutMs
pool.idleTimeoutMs 控制空闲连接保留时间。池在维护周期回收闲置连接，不因此取消活跃事务。调大此值不能解决连接池排队请求的 acquire timeout。
  5. d60--c000 · 分级 0
    # 正式员工离职
大陆团队正式员工的工作区访问在最后工作日 20:00 关闭，由直属经理确认。该时间不适用于承包商。人事归档与访问关闭是独立步骤。

  1. d21--c000 · 分级 3
    # Webhook signature v2
Validate the X-Meridian-Signature-V2 header with HMAC-SHA256 over the raw request bytes. A JSON parse-and-reserialize step changes the signed bytes. Header names are case-insensitive, but the V2 suffix identifies a different scheme from the legacy header.
  2. d36--c000 · 分级 0
    # Service-to-service credentials
A backend integration should use a service identity with a dedicated token. Do not make a human teammate use that shared integration identity to perform administrative work. Personal invitations and machine credentials serve different purposes.
  3. d13--c000 · 分级 0
    # PAY_SETTLE_V2 consumer
When moving PAY_SETTLE_V2 to another consumer group, commit the final source offset before disabling the old group. Start the new group from that recorded offset. This queue carries settlement events; replaying from earliest can duplicate external settlement requests.
  4. d37--c000 · 分级 0
    # Emergency account recovery
If the sole workspace owner cannot sign in, use the verified recovery process through support. An already signed-in owner who wants help with administration should use normal delegated access rather than emergency recovery.
  5. d95--c000 · 分级 0
    Incident 950: a staging deployment used an old image and was reverted before promotion. The summary says production was unaffected. It mentions restore approvals only as an unrelated checklist item; no customer database restore occurred.

    # Webhook signature v2
Validate the X-Meridian-Signature-V2 header with HMAC-SHA256 over the raw request bytes. A JSON parse-and-reserialize step changes the signed bytes. Header names are case-insensitive, but the V2 suffix identifies a different scheme from the legacy header.
  2. d13--c000 · 分级 0
    # PAY_SETTLE_V2 consumer
When moving PAY_SETTLE_V2 to another consumer group, commit the final source offset before disabling the old group. Start the new group from that recorded offset. This queue carries settlement events; replaying from earliest can duplicate external settlement requests.
  3. d95--c000 · 分级 0
    Incident 950: a staging deployment used an old image and was reverted before promotion. The summary says production was unaffected. It mentions restore approvals only as an unrelated checklist item; no customer database restore occurred.
  4. d53--c000 · 分级 0
    # Managed-key exports
A data export encrypted with a managed workspace key can be downloaded only by users with Export Operator permission. Workspace Viewer permission does not grant export download rights even if the viewer can see the export job status.
  5. d54--c000 · 分级 0
    # Public sample exports
Public sample exports contain synthetic data and can be downloaded by a Workspace Viewer. They do not use the managed workspace key. Do not infer rights for customer-data exports from the public sample workflow.

    # Webhook signature v1 archive
Legacy X-Meridian-Signature uses HMAC-SHA1 over the canonical field sequence. V1 is available only for tenants explicitly left on the legacy endpoint. It is not the algorithm for X-Meridian-Signature-V2.
  3. d23--c000 · 分级 0
    # Outbound webhook responses
X-Meridian-Request-ID is a correlation identifier, not an authentication proof. Echo it in support tickets. Neither comparing this ID nor trusting a successful HTTP status verifies the inbound signature.
  4. d03--c000 · 分级 0
    # Relay client 4.1 / RC-1047
Archived workaround for release 4.1 only: remove the cached API secret and issue a replacement secret. The workspace-binding implementation changed in release 4.2. Do not apply this archived workaround to a newer client.
  5. d36--c000 · 分级 0
    # Service-to-service credentials

  1. d25--c000 · 分级 3
    # Atlas alert acknowledgement
Acknowledge marks an alert as owned; it does not mute future notifications. To stop pages during planned work, create a time-bounded silence with a matching service selector. The silence must have an expiry time and a maintenance-ticket link.
  2. d65--c000 · 分级 0
    # Deployment controller v3 rollback
Controller v3 keeps the previous image available for 48 hours after promotion. Rollback within that window uses deploy revert. After the window expires, submit a new deployment of the desired image. This release replaced the v2 twelve-hour window.
  3. d10--c000 · 分级 0
    # RX-802 mirrored storage
For RX-802 with a steady amber mirror LED and code M12, reseat the inter-controller cable during the approved maintenance window. This older model has no standby cache battery. Do not follow RX-820 battery instructions.
  4. d11--c000 · 分级 0
    # RX-820 flashing amber
A flashing amber mirror LED means synchronization is active. Wait for synchronization to finish before maintenance. Do not replace the battery on this signal alone; steady amber M12 is a separate fault.
  5. d36--c000 · 分级 0
    # Service-to-service credentials
A backend integration should use a service identity with a dedicated token. Do not make a human teammate use that shared integration identity to perform administrative work. Personal invitations and machine credentials serve different purposes.

恢复前先用最后一个已确认结算编号核对银行回执，再开放新任务入口。只看到消费者进程启动不能视为恢复完成。
  3. d40--c000 · 分级 0
    # 日志读取权限
查询返回权限拒绝时，确认日志项目成员关系。重新创建日志文件不会授予读取权限。时间范围为空和权限拒绝必须分开诊断。
  4. d93--c000 · 分级 0
    # 大陆员工数据交接
离职前将工作文件转移到团队共享空间，由直属经理确认可读取后再擦除个人设备。资产签收单不能替代数据交接确认，未经确认不得提前擦盘。
  5. d94--c000 · 分级 0
    # 访客离场
访客离开办公室时把临时门禁卡投入前台回收箱即可，不需要员工资产签收单。访客流程不涉及员工电脑擦除或团队工作文件交接。

BGE_M3_VECTOR · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d30--c000 · 分级 0
    # Portal interrupted uploads
If the transfer ends before a receipt is issued, resume using the saved upload token. A network failure before completion differs from a completed upload waiting for malware scanning. Do not use an absent library entry as proof of a failed transfer.
  2. d29--c000 · 分级 3
    # Portal upload recovery
A completed upload can remain invisible while malware scanning is pending. Keep the upload receipt and check the scan state before sending the same file again. Repeated uploads create independent scan jobs; they do not accelerate the original one.
  3. d31--c000 · 分级 0
    # Portal rejected file
A file with scan state REJECTED is not published. Ask the security team to inspect the finding; do not rename the file to evade scanning. Pending means the scan has not finished and is not itself a rejection.
  4. d82--c003 · 分级 0
    # 紧急访问：撤销
服务恢复后主动撤销会话，即使45分钟尚未用完。证据日志放入审计存储，不能留在个人电脑。

问题、证据和完整标注复核
KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d87--c000 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：准备
维护 PAY_SETTLE_V2 前通知财务值班，暂停接收新的结算任务。已经进入外部提交阶段的任务不能直接杀进程。
  2. d87--c002 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：恢复
恢复前先用最后一个已确认结算编号核对银行回执，再开放新任务入口。只看到消费者进程启动不能视为恢复完成。
  3. d17--c000 · 分级 0
    # pool.acquireTimeoutMs
数据库连接池参数 pool.acquireTimeoutMs 控制调用方等待空闲连接的最长时间，单位为毫秒。它不终止已经执行的 SQL。等待超时应检查连接泄漏和并发量，而不是直接增加语句超时。
  4. d05--c000 · 分级 0
    # Edge 中国区生产环境 / E-218
边缘采集器出现 E-218 表示离线队列磁盘已满。先导出未发送记录，再清理已确认送达的批次。禁止直接删除整个 spool 目录；这样会丢失尚未送达的记录。处理后检查队列水位。
  5. d32--c000 · 分级 3
    # 消费者延迟与扩容
工作进程 CPU 很低但待处理消息持续增加时，先检查消费是否被下游限流阻塞。查看 retry-after 和下游并发配额，再决定是否扩容。盲目增加消费者可能加重限流，低 CPU 不等于队列没有工作。

CURRENT_VECTOR · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d32--c000 · 分级 3
    # 消费者延迟与扩容
工作进程 CPU 很低但待处理消息持续增加时，先检查消费是否被下游限流阻塞。查看 retry-after 和下游并发配额，再决定是否扩容。盲目增加消费者可能加重限流，低 CPU 不等于队列没有工作。
  2. d34--c000 · 分级 0
    # 消息堆积与空闲分区
单分区键倾斜会造成一个消费者繁忙而其他消费者空闲。先检查分区分布，调整业务键策略。增加同一分区的消费者不能获得额外并行度；这与下游服务限流是不同原因。
  3. d82--c000 · 分级 0
    # 紧急访问：适用条件
大陆生产值班仅在正常审批系统不可用且客户服务中断时使用紧急访问。测试演练不能借此获取真实客户数据。
  4. d33--c000 · 分级 0
    # 消费者计算瓶颈
CPU 持续接近满载、没有下游限流且处理时间稳定时，可增加分区消费者数量。扩容不得超过可并行分区数。这一建议不适用于 CPU 低且大量请求被下游拒绝的情况。

工作进程 CPU 很低但待处理消息持续增加时，先检查消费是否被下游限流阻塞。查看 retry-after 和下游并发配额，再决定是否扩容。盲目增加消费者可能加重限流，低 CPU 不等于队列没有工作。
  3. d87--c002 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：恢复
恢复前先用最后一个已确认结算编号核对银行回执，再开放新任务入口。只看到消费者进程启动不能视为恢复完成。
  4. d05--c000 · 分级 0
    # Edge 中国区生产环境 / E-218
边缘采集器出现 E-218 表示离线队列磁盘已满。先导出未发送记录，再清理已确认送达的批次。禁止直接删除整个 spool 目录；这样会丢失尚未送达的记录。处理后检查队列水位。
  5. d17--c000 · 分级 0
    # pool.acquireTimeoutMs
数据库连接池参数 pool.acquireTimeoutMs 控制调用方等待空闲连接的最长时间，单位为毫秒。它不终止已经执行的 SQL。等待超时应检查连接泄漏和并发量，而不是直接增加语句超时。

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d35--c000 · 分级 3
    # Delegated administrator access
When a teammate only needs to help administer a workspace, invite them with a scoped role instead of sharing your password or API token. Delegated access has its own audit identity and can be revoked without rotating your personal credentials.
  2. d37--c000 · 分级 0
    # Emergency account recovery
If the sole workspace owner cannot sign in, use the verified recovery process through support. An already signed-in owner who wants help with administration should use normal delegated access rather than emergency recovery.
  3. d84--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU regulated export
An EU regulated-data export needs compliance approval before generation. The approval is attached to the export job; ordinary workspace administrator rights are not sufficient. The generated archive must use the tenant-managed encryption key.
  4. d01--c000 · 分级 0
    # Relay client 4.2 / RC-1047
Meridian support note, reviewed 2026-09-14. RC-1047 means the saved workspace binding belongs to another tenant. Run relay workspace unlink, then authenticate against the intended tenant. Rotating the API secret does not remove the binding. Preserve the request ID in the support ticket.
  5. d22--c000 · 分级 0
    # Webhook signature v1 archive
Legacy X-Meridian-Signature uses HMAC-SHA1 over the canonical field sequence. V1 is available only for tenants explicitly left on the legacy endpoint. It is not the algorithm for X-Meridian-Signature-V2.

  3. d25--c000 · 分级 0
    # Atlas alert acknowledgement
Acknowledge marks an alert as owned; it does not mute future notifications. To stop pages during planned work, create a time-bounded silence with a matching service selector. The silence must have an expiry time and a maintenance-ticket link.
  4. d41--c000 · 分级 0
    # Restore versus retention
Extending backup retention affects future expiry decisions; it does not resurrect snapshots that have already been deleted. To recover a deleted snapshot, check for an independently retained export. Without another copy the retention setting cannot recover the old bytes.
  5. d29--c000 · 分级 0
    # Portal upload recovery

    # Trial support

  2. d87--c000 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：准备
维护 PAY_SETTLE_V2 前通知财务值班，暂停接收新的结算任务。已经进入外部提交阶段的任务不能直接杀进程。
  3. d19--c000 · 分级 0
    # pool.idleTimeoutMs
pool.idleTimeoutMs 控制空闲连接保留时间。池在维护周期回收闲置连接，不因此取消活跃事务。调大此值不能解决连接池排队请求的 acquire timeout。
  4. d17--c000 · 分级 0
    # pool.acquireTimeoutMs
数据库连接池参数 pool.acquireTimeoutMs 控制调用方等待空闲连接的最长时间，单位为毫秒。它不终止已经执行的 SQL。等待超时应检查连接泄漏和并发量，而不是直接增加语句超时。
  5. d94--c000 · 分级 0
    # 访客离场

BGE_M3_VECTOR · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d41--c000 · 分级 3
    # Restore versus retention
Extending backup retention affects future expiry decisions; it does not resurrect snapshots that have already been deleted. To recover a deleted snapshot, check for an independently retained export. Without another copy the retention setting cannot recover the old bytes.
  2. d80--c003 · 分级 0
    # Production restore: records
Keep the restore change ticket and both approval records for 400 days after closure. Snapshot byte retention is a separate policy; deleting the temporary snapshot does not permit deleting the approval trail.

    # Backup frequency
Increasing backup frequency reduces the interval between new snapshots. It does not extend existing snapshot retention or recreate deleted backups. The schedule and the retention policy are separate settings.

  1. d44--c000 · 分级 3
    # 中国区生产 API：团队版
团队版租户批量导出上限为每次 5000 行，按租户汇总而不是按用户计算。企业版和测试环境使用不同配额；拆成多个用户调用不会提高团队版租户配额。
  2. d45--c000 · 分级 0
    # 中国区生产 API：企业版
企业版租户批量导出上限为每次 50000 行。该额度不能用于团队版客户报价；客户升级须完成订单生效流程。两个版本使用相同的导出接口。
  3. d46--c000 · 分级 0
    # 中国区测试 API
隔离测试环境的批量导出上限为每次 100000 行，用于验证大文件流程。测试额度不代表生产环境团队版或企业版的额度。
  4. d05--c000 · 分级 0
    # Edge 中国区生产环境 / E-218
边缘采集器出现 E-218 表示离线队列磁盘已满。先导出未发送记录，再清理已确认送达的批次。禁止直接删除整个 spool 目录；这样会丢失尚未送达的记录。处理后检查队列水位。
  5. d06--c000 · 分级 0
    # Edge 中国区生产环境 / E-281
E-281 表示采集器时钟偏差超过五分钟。同步 NTP，确认时区和宿主机时间后重试。此错误与离线队列容量无关，不应清理 spool。

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d48--c000 · 分级 0
    # US production support access
For a US production tenant, approval by the US duty lead is sufficient for a two-hour diagnostic session. This procedure does not authorize access to EU tenant data. Region refers to the tenant's data region, not the engineer's location.
  2. d47--c000 · 分级 3
    # EU production support access
For an EU production tenant, customer-data access requires approval by the customer owner and the EU duty lead. A US duty lead cannot substitute for the EU duty lead. Access expires after two hours.
  3. d49--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU sandbox support access
Synthetic-data sandbox access requires only a sandbox owner approval. No production customer data may be copied into the sandbox to bypass the EU production approval procedure.
  4. d80--c001 · 分级 0
    # Production restore: approval
Before a production restore starts, obtain approvals from the service owner and the data-protection lead. The operator cannot approve their own request. Store both approvals in the change ticket.
  5. d81--c000 · 分级 0
    # Sandbox restore
Synthetic-data sandbox restores need only the sandbox owner approval. Exercise tickets can be removed after 14 days. Neither the single approval nor fourteen-day record policy applies to production restores.

    # 大陆采购：硬件

  4. d60--c000 · 分级 0
    # 正式员工离职
大陆团队正式员工的工作区访问在最后工作日 20:00 关闭，由直属经理确认。该时间不适用于承包商。人事归档与访问关闭是独立步骤。
  5. d45--c000 · 分级 0

    # 大陆采购：软件订阅
软件订阅无论金额大小都需要安全评审和预算负责人批准。硬件 8000 元阈值不适用软件。免费试用涉及公司数据时也需要安全评审。
  3. d52--c000 · 分级 0
    # 大陆维修报销
已批准设备的紧急维修不超过 2000 元可先维修后补报，需保留维修单。该例外不适用于购买新硬件或软件订阅。
  4. d93--c000 · 分级 0
    # 大陆员工数据交接
离职前将工作文件转移到团队共享空间，由直属经理确认可读取后再擦除个人设备。资产签收单不能替代数据交接确认，未经确认不得提前擦盘。
  5. d44--c000 · 分级 0
    # 中国区生产 API：团队版
团队版租户批量导出上限为每次 5000 行，按租户汇总而不是按用户计算。企业版和测试环境使用不同配额；拆成多个用户调用不会提高团队版租户配额。

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d53--c000 · 分级 3
    # Managed-key exports
A data export encrypted with a managed workspace key can be downloaded only by users with Export Operator permission. Workspace Viewer permission does not grant export download rights even if the viewer can see the export job status.
  2. d54--c000 · 分级 0
    # Public sample exports
Public sample exports contain synthetic data and can be downloaded by a Workspace Viewer. They do not use the managed workspace key. Do not infer rights for customer-data exports from the public sample workflow.
  3. d55--c000 · 分级 0
    # Export Auditor role
Export Auditor may inspect job metadata and audit events but cannot download managed-key exports. Export Operator grants download permission. The two roles should not be treated as synonyms.
  4. d85--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU regulated export delivery
Deliver an approved EU regulated-data export only through an expiring download link, valid for six hours. Do not email the archive as an attachment. Delivery rules do not replace compliance approval before generation.
  5. d84--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU regulated export
An EU regulated-data export needs compliance approval before generation. The approval is attached to the export job; ordinary workspace administrator rights are not sufficient. The generated archive must use the tenant-managed encryption key.

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d56--c000 · 分级 3
    # Billing cutoff time zone
Singapore tenants close their billing month at 00:00 Asia/Singapore on the first day of the next month. The engineer's browser timezone does not change this cutoff. Sydney tenants use a separate regional schedule.
  2. d58--c000 · 分级 0
    # Dashboard display time
The browser can display event timestamps in the viewer's local timezone. Display preferences do not change billing-period assignment. A screenshot showing Sydney time is not sufficient to infer the tenant's billing region.
  3. d57--c000 · 分级 0
    # Sydney billing cutoff
Sydney tenants close the billing month at 00:00 Australia/Sydney on the first day of the next month, observing local daylight saving. This does not define the Singapore tenant cutoff.
  4. d48--c000 · 分级 0
    # US production support access
For a US production tenant, approval by the US duty lead is sufficient for a two-hour diagnostic session. This procedure does not authorize access to EU tenant data. Region refers to the tenant's data region, not the engineer's location.
  5. d84--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU regulated export
An EU regulated-data export needs compliance approval before generation. The approval is attached to the export job; ordinary workspace administrator rights are not sufficient. The generated archive must use the tenant-managed encryption key.

For a US production tenant, approval by the US duty lead is sufficient for a two-hour diagnostic session. This procedure does not authorize access to EU tenant data. Region refers to the tenant's data region, not the engineer's location.
  5. d84--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU regulated export
An EU regulated-data export needs compliance approval before generation. The approval is attached to the export job; ordinary workspace administrator rights are not sufficient. The generated archive must use the tenant-managed encryption key.
CURRENT_VECTOR · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d56--c000 · 分级 3
    # Billing cutoff time zone
Singapore tenants close their billing month at 00:00 Asia/Singapore on the first day of the next month. The engineer's browser timezone does not change this cutoff. Sydney tenants use a separate regional schedule.
  2. d57--c000 · 分级 0
    # Sydney billing cutoff
Sydney tenants close the billing month at 00:00 Australia/Sydney on the first day of the next month, observing local daylight saving. This does not define the Singapore tenant cutoff.
  3. d58--c000 · 分级 0
    # Dashboard display time
The browser can display event timestamps in the viewer's local timezone. Display preferences do not change billing-period assignment. A screenshot showing Sydney time is not sufficient to infer the tenant's billing region.
  4. d78--c000 · 分级 0
    # Monthly subscription renewals
Monthly renewals are non-refundable after the renewal charge settles, except duplicate billing. The annual-plan seven-day and fourteen-day windows do not apply to monthly renewals.
  5. d90--c000 · 分级 0
    # Service credit claim evidence
A Meridian enterprise service-credit claim must include tenant ID, affected UTC intervals and incident references. A dashboard screenshot alone is insufficient. Support uses these fields to verify the measured availability for the claimed month.

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d59--c000 · 分级 3
    # 承包商离场
大陆团队承包商合同结束当天 18:00 前关闭工作区访问，交由外包负责人确认。正式员工离职流程和账号保留期限不同。审计记录不随访问关闭而删除。
  2. d60--c000 · 分级 0
    # 正式员工离职
大陆团队正式员工的工作区访问在最后工作日 20:00 关闭，由直属经理确认。该时间不适用于承包商。人事归档与访问关闭是独立步骤。
  3. d61--c000 · 分级 0
    # 访客账号到期
大陆办公室访客账号在访问申请结束时自动失效，默认当天 17:00。访客不是项目承包商，不能用访客到期时间代替承包商离场流程。
  4. d93--c000 · 分级 0
    # 大陆员工数据交接
离职前将工作文件转移到团队共享空间，由直属经理确认可读取后再擦除个人设备。资产签收单不能替代数据交接确认，未经确认不得提前擦盘。
  5. d94--c000 · 分级 0
    # 访客离场
访客离开办公室时把临时门禁卡投入前台回收箱即可，不需要员工资产签收单。访客流程不涉及员工电脑擦除或团队工作文件交接。

    # Invoice dispute policy / effective 2026-08-01
From 1 August 2026, new invoice disputes must be opened within 45 calendar days of invoice issue. Disputes opened before that date remain under the older 30-day rule. This change does not extend the payment due date.
  2. d63--c000 · 分级 0
    # Invoice dispute policy / archived
From March through July 2026, an invoice dispute had to be opened within 30 calendar days of issue. This page is archived; the August policy supersedes it for newly opened disputes. Payment terms are separate.
  3. d64--c000 · 分级 0
    # Invoice payment terms
Payment is due 15 calendar days after invoice issue unless the signed order states otherwise. Dispute-submission windows do not automatically suspend or extend the due date. Do not confuse the 15-day payment period with the dispute period.
  4. d71--c000 · 分级 0
    # API release / 2026-10-01
New export jobs submitted from 1 October 2026 return a job handle immediately; poll GET /exports/{id} for completion. Jobs submitted before the cutover retain the synchronous response contract. Request submission time determines the contract.
  5. d77--c000 · 分级 0
    # Subscription refund transition
Annual plans purchased on or after 1 June 2026 have a fourteen-day cancellation window. Earlier annual purchases retain a seven-day window. Monthly renewals are excluded from both rules. Use purchase date, not cancellation date, to select the rule.

  1. d65--c000 · 分级 3
    # Deployment controller v3 rollback
Controller v3 keeps the previous image available for 48 hours after promotion. Rollback within that window uses deploy revert. After the window expires, submit a new deployment of the desired image. This release replaced the v2 twelve-hour window.
  2. d66--c000 · 分级 0
    # Deployment controller v2 archive
Controller v2 permits deploy revert for twelve hours after promotion. A newer v3 controller uses a different retention window. The archived v2 operations guide is retained for tenants not yet migrated.
  3. d67--c000 · 分级 0
    # Cancel a pending deployment
A deployment still waiting for promotion can be cancelled without invoking rollback. Cancellation does not restore a version that has already been promoted. Check the deployment state before choosing cancel or revert.
  4. d95--c000 · 分级 0
    Incident 950: a staging deployment used an old image and was reverted before promotion. The summary says production was unaffected. It mentions restore approvals only as an unrelated checklist item; no customer database restore occurred.
  5. d09--c000 · 分级 0
    # RX-820 mirrored storage
For the RX-820 controller, a steady amber mirror LED with code M12 requires replacing the standby cache battery. Keep the active controller online. Flashing amber has a different meaning and is not covered by this procedure.

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d72--c000 · 分级 0
    # Legacy synchronous export
Before 1 October 2026, POST /exports returned the completed download URL in the response. Clients submitting new jobs after the cutover must use the asynchronous job-handle contract even if the client itself was installed earlier.
  2. d71--c000 · 分级 3
    # API release / 2026-10-01
New export jobs submitted from 1 October 2026 return a job handle immediately; poll GET /exports/{id} for completion. Jobs submitted before the cutover retain the synchronous response contract. Request submission time determines the contract.
  3. d73--c000 · 分级 0
    # Import API
POST /imports has used asynchronous job handles since January 2026. Import status and export status have different endpoints. The export October cutover did not change the import contract.
  4. d13--c000 · 分级 0
    # PAY_SETTLE_V2 consumer
When moving PAY_SETTLE_V2 to another consumer group, commit the final source offset before disabling the old group. Start the new group from that recorded offset. This queue carries settlement events; replaying from earliest can duplicate external settlement requests.
  5. d55--c000 · 分级 0
    # Export Auditor role
Export Auditor may inspect job metadata and audit events but cannot download managed-key exports. Export Operator grants download permission. The two roles should not be treated as synonyms.

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d74--c000 · 分级 3
    # 证书轮换新规
从2026年7月15日起签发的设备证书有效期为90天；此前签发的180天证书继续按原到期日有效。新规不追溯缩短既有证书。是否适用新规取决于签发日期，而非客户端升级日期。
  2. d75--c000 · 分级 0
    # 证书有效期旧规
2026年7月15日之前签发的设备证书有效期为180天。后续新签发证书使用新规。旧版手册保留用于核对存量证书，不能据此给8月新证书设置180天。
  3. d76--c000 · 分级 0
    # 泄露证书撤销
证书私钥疑似泄露时应立即撤销，不等待90天或180天有效期结束。证书轮换周期不限制紧急撤销。撤销后须重新签发并部署凭据。
  4. d52--c000 · 分级 0
    # 大陆维修报销
已批准设备的紧急维修不超过 2000 元可先维修后补报，需保留维修单。该例外不适用于购买新硬件或软件订阅。
  5. d38--c000 · 分级 0
    # 工作区日志滚动
应用重启后历史日志看起来消失，但持久卷还在时，先检查日志轮转后的归档文件和查询时间范围。新的活动日志文件从空白开始不代表归档被删除。不要立即把持久卷重新初始化。

    # Relay client 4.2 / RC-1074
RC-1074 indicates that the client certificate has expired. Renew the certificate through Device Trust, restart the client, and retry. Unlinking the workspace does not renew a certificate. This note does not apply to expired user sessions.
  5. d77--c000 · 分级 0
    # Subscription refund transition
Annual plans purchased on or after 1 June 2026 have a fourteen-day cancellation window. Earlier annual purchases retain a seven-day window. Monthly renewals are excluded from both rules. Use purchase date, not cancellation date, to select the rule.

  1. d77--c000 · 分级 3
    # Subscription refund transition
Annual plans purchased on or after 1 June 2026 have a fourteen-day cancellation window. Earlier annual purchases retain a seven-day window. Monthly renewals are excluded from both rules. Use purchase date, not cancellation date, to select the rule.

Annual plans purchased before June 2026 had a seven-day cancellation window. Later annual purchases follow the transition notice. A June cancellation of a May purchase does not make it a new June purchase.

    # Production restore: records
Keep the restore change ticket and both approval records for 400 days after closure. Snapshot byte retention is a separate policy; deleting the temporary snapshot does not permit deleting the approval trail.
  2. d80--c001 · 分级 2
    # Production restore: approval
Before a production restore starts, obtain approvals from the service owner and the data-protection lead. The operator cannot approve their own request. Store both approvals in the change ticket.
  3. d95--c000 · 分级 0
    Incident 950: a staging deployment used an old image and was reverted before promotion. The summary says production was unaffected. It mentions restore approvals only as an unrelated checklist item; no customer database restore occurred.

    # Sandbox restore
Synthetic-data sandbox restores need only the sandbox owner approval. Exercise tickets can be removed after 14 days. Neither the single approval nor fourteen-day record policy applies to production restores.

    # 紧急访问：审计
访问结束后24小时内补交操作记录和故障编号，由安全团队复核。关闭访问不是完成审计，故障仍未解决也不能无限延期。
  2. d83--c000 · 分级 0
    # 测试演练访问
没有客户数据的演练环境可由演练负责人授权两小时访问，三天内交演练总结。生产紧急访问的时限与审计规则不同，不能使用演练时限。
  3. d82--c000 · 分级 0
    # 紧急访问：适用条件
大陆生产值班仅在正常审批系统不可用且客户服务中断时使用紧急访问。测试演练不能借此获取真实客户数据。
  4. d82--c001 · 分级 2
    # 紧急访问：授权
大陆生产紧急访问由当班负责人授予，最长持续45分钟。到期必须重新审批，不能通过重新登录延长。操作人不得给自己授权。
  5. d59--c000 · 分级 0
    # 承包商离场
大陆团队承包商合同结束当天 18:00 前关闭工作区访问，交由外包负责人确认。正式员工离职流程和账号保留期限不同。审计记录不随访问关闭而删除。

    # 紧急访问：审计
访问结束后24小时内补交操作记录和故障编号，由安全团队复核。关闭访问不是完成审计，故障仍未解决也不能无限延期。
  3. d82--c000 · 分级 0
    # 紧急访问：适用条件
大陆生产值班仅在正常审批系统不可用且客户服务中断时使用紧急访问。测试演练不能借此获取真实客户数据。
  4. d83--c000 · 分级 0
    # 测试演练访问
没有客户数据的演练环境可由演练负责人授权两小时访问，三天内交演练总结。生产紧急访问的时限与审计规则不同，不能使用演练时限。
  5. d82--c003 · 分级 0
    # 紧急访问：撤销
服务恢复后主动撤销会话，即使45分钟尚未用完。证据日志放入审计存储，不能留在个人电脑。

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d85--c000 · 分级 2
    # EU regulated export delivery
Deliver an approved EU regulated-data export only through an expiring download link, valid for six hours. Do not email the archive as an attachment. Delivery rules do not replace compliance approval before generation.
  2. d84--c000 · 分级 2
    # EU regulated export
An EU regulated-data export needs compliance approval before generation. The approval is attached to the export job; ordinary workspace administrator rights are not sufficient. The generated archive must use the tenant-managed encryption key.
  3. d86--c000 · 分级 0
    # Synthetic sample export delivery
Synthetic sample archives can be emailed to workshop participants and do not need compliance approval. They contain no regulated data. These workshop rules do not authorize emailing an EU regulated-data archive.
  4. d49--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU sandbox support access
Synthetic-data sandbox access requires only a sandbox owner approval. No production customer data may be copied into the sandbox to bypass the EU production approval procedure.
  5. d47--c000 · 分级 0
    # EU production support access
For an EU production tenant, customer-data access requires approval by the customer owner and the EU duty lead. A US duty lead cannot substitute for the EU duty lead. Access expires after two hours.

c28

问题、证据和完整标注复核
KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d87--c002 · 分级 2
    # 支付结算维护：恢复
恢复前先用最后一个已确认结算编号核对银行回执，再开放新任务入口。只看到消费者进程启动不能视为恢复完成。
  2. d87--c003 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：例外
如果银行接口不可用，保持入口暂停并升级财务值班。不要反复重放未知结果的提交，以免重复扣款。
  3. d87--c000 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：准备
维护 PAY_SETTLE_V2 前通知财务值班，暂停接收新的结算任务。已经进入外部提交阶段的任务不能直接杀进程。
  4. d87--c001 · 分级 2
    # 支付结算维护：排空
暂停接收后，等待 in_flight_settlements 降到0再停止消费者。队列长度为0并不证明外部银行提交已结束。
  5. d13--c000 · 分级 0
    # PAY_SETTLE_V2 consumer
When moving PAY_SETTLE_V2 to another consumer group, commit the final source offset before disabling the old group. Start the new group from that recorded offset. This queue carries settlement events; replaying from earliest can duplicate external settlement requests.
KEYWORD_FILENAME · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d87--c002 · 分级 2
    # 支付结算维护：恢复
恢复前先用最后一个已确认结算编号核对银行回执，再开放新任务入口。只看到消费者进程启动不能视为恢复完成。
  2. d87--c003 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：例外
如果银行接口不可用，保持入口暂停并升级财务值班。不要反复重放未知结果的提交，以免重复扣款。
  3. d87--c000 · 分级 0
    # 支付结算维护：准备
维护 PAY_SETTLE_V2 前通知财务值班，暂停接收新的结算任务。已经进入外部提交阶段的任务不能直接杀进程。
  4. d87--c001 · 分级 2
    # 支付结算维护：排空
暂停接收后，等待 in_flight_settlements 降到0再停止消费者。队列长度为0并不证明外部银行提交已结束。
  5. d13--c000 · 分级 0
    # PAY_SETTLE_V2 consumer
When moving PAY_SETTLE_V2 to another consumer group, commit the final source offset before disabling the old group. Start the new group from that recorded offset. This queue carries settlement events; replaying from earliest can duplicate external settlement requests.

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d90--c000 · 分级 2
    # Service credit claim evidence
A Meridian enterprise service-credit claim must include tenant ID, affected UTC intervals and incident references. A dashboard screenshot alone is insufficient. Support uses these fields to verify the measured availability for the claimed month.
  2. d89--c000 · 分级 2
    # Enterprise service credits
Enterprise tenants may request a service credit when measured monthly availability is below 99.9%. Open the claim within ten calendar days after the affected month ends. Planned maintenance excluded by contract does not enter measured downtime.
  3. d58--c000 · 分级 0
    # Dashboard display time
The browser can display event timestamps in the viewer's local timezone. Display preferences do not change billing-period assignment. A screenshot showing Sydney time is not sufficient to infer the tenant's billing region.
  4. d91--c000 · 分级 0
    # Trial support
Trial tenants receive best-effort incident support but no contractual service credits. The enterprise availability threshold does not create a credit entitlement for a trial. Trial users may still submit incident reports.
  5. d80--c000 · 分级 0
    # Production restore: eligibility
A Meridian production restore must name the recovery point and customer tenant. Sandbox exercises use a different process; an exercise ticket cannot authorize a production restore.

问题、证据和完整标注复核
KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 1.0000
  1. d92--c000 · 分级 2
    # 大陆员工设备归还
离职员工在最后工作日把电脑和门禁卡交给资产管理员，取得签收单。远程员工应提前安排签收快递；快递发出不等于资产已经签收。
  2. d93--c000 · 分级 2
    # 大陆员工数据交接
离职前将工作文件转移到团队共享空间，由直属经理确认可读取后再擦除个人设备。资产签收单不能替代数据交接确认，未经确认不得提前擦盘。
  3. d94--c000 · 分级 0
    # 访客离场
访客离开办公室时把临时门禁卡投入前台回收箱即可，不需要员工资产签收单。访客流程不涉及员工电脑擦除或团队工作文件交接。
  4. d60--c000 · 分级 0
    # 正式员工离职
大陆团队正式员工的工作区访问在最后工作日 20:00 关闭，由直属经理确认。该时间不适用于承包商。人事归档与访问关闭是独立步骤。
  5. d59--c000 · 分级 0
    # 承包商离场
大陆团队承包商合同结束当天 18:00 前关闭工作区访问，交由外包负责人确认。正式员工离职流程和账号保留期限不同。审计记录不随访问关闭而删除。

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 —
  1. d37--c000 · 分级 0
    # Emergency account recovery
If the sole workspace owner cannot sign in, use the verified recovery process through support. An already signed-in owner who wants help with administration should use normal delegated access rather than emergency recovery.
  2. d01--c000 · 分级 0
    # Relay client 4.2 / RC-1047
Meridian support note, reviewed 2026-09-14. RC-1047 means the saved workspace binding belongs to another tenant. Run relay workspace unlink, then authenticate against the intended tenant. Rotating the API secret does not remove the binding. Preserve the request ID in the support ticket.
  3. d80--c000 · 分级 0
    # Production restore: eligibility
A Meridian production restore must name the recovery point and customer tenant. Sandbox exercises use a different process; an exercise ticket cannot authorize a production restore.
  4. d03--c000 · 分级 0
    # Relay client 4.1 / RC-1047
Archived workaround for release 4.1 only: remove the cached API secret and issue a replacement secret. The workspace-binding implementation changed in release 4.2. Do not apply this archived workaround to a newer client.
  5. d04--c000 · 分级 0
    # Relay gateway / RC-1047
In the gateway process, RC-1047 reports an upstream DNS lookup failure. Check the resolver configuration and the private DNS zone. Gateway error codes and desktop client error codes use separate namespaces.

    # 大陆采购：硬件

    # 中国区生产 API：企业版

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 —
  1. d58--c000 · 分级 0
    # Dashboard display time
The browser can display event timestamps in the viewer's local timezone. Display preferences do not change billing-period assignment. A screenshot showing Sydney time is not sufficient to infer the tenant's billing region.
  2. d57--c000 · 分级 0
    # Sydney billing cutoff
Sydney tenants close the billing month at 00:00 Australia/Sydney on the first day of the next month, observing local daylight saving. This does not define the Singapore tenant cutoff.
  3. d48--c000 · 分级 0
    # US production support access
For a US production tenant, approval by the US duty lead is sufficient for a two-hour diagnostic session. This procedure does not authorize access to EU tenant data. Region refers to the tenant's data region, not the engineer's location.
  4. d89--c000 · 分级 0
    # Enterprise service credits
Enterprise tenants may request a service credit when measured monthly availability is below 99.9%. Open the claim within ten calendar days after the affected month ends. Planned maintenance excluded by contract does not enter measured downtime.
  5. d91--c000 · 分级 0
    # Trial support
Trial tenants receive best-effort incident support but no contractual service credits. The enterprise availability threshold does not create a credit entitlement for a trial. Trial users may still submit incident reports.

    # pool.idleTimeoutMs

  3. d22--c000 · 分级 0
    # Webhook signature v1 archive
Legacy X-Meridian-Signature uses HMAC-SHA1 over the canonical field sequence. V1 is available only for tenants explicitly left on the legacy endpoint. It is not the algorithm for X-Meridian-Signature-V2.
  4. d80--c000 · 分级 0
    # Production restore: eligibility
A Meridian production restore must name the recovery point and customer tenant. Sandbox exercises use a different process; an exercise ticket cannot authorize a production restore.
  5. d21--c000 · 分级 0
    # Webhook signature v2
Validate the X-Meridian-Signature-V2 header with HMAC-SHA256 over the raw request bytes. A JSON parse-and-reserialize step changes the signed bytes. Header names are case-insensitive, but the V2 suffix identifies a different scheme from the legacy header.

KEYWORD_BODY · Recall@5 —
  1. d60--c000 · 分级 0
    # 正式员工离职
大陆团队正式员工的工作区访问在最后工作日 20:00 关闭，由直属经理确认。该时间不适用于承包商。人事归档与访问关闭是独立步骤。
  2. d59--c000 · 分级 0
    # 承包商离场
大陆团队承包商合同结束当天 18:00 前关闭工作区访问，交由外包负责人确认。正式员工离职流程和账号保留期限不同。审计记录不随访问关闭而删除。
  3. d93--c000 · 分级 0
    # 大陆员工数据交接
离职前将工作文件转移到团队共享空间，由直属经理确认可读取后再擦除个人设备。资产签收单不能替代数据交接确认，未经确认不得提前擦盘。
  4. d92--c000 · 分级 0
    # 大陆员工设备归还
离职员工在最后工作日把电脑和门禁卡交给资产管理员，取得签收单。远程员工应提前安排签收快递；快递发出不等于资产已经签收。
  5. d94--c000 · 分级 0
    # 访客离场
访客离开办公室时把临时门禁卡投入前台回收箱即可，不需要员工资产签收单。访客流程不涉及员工电脑擦除或团队工作文件交接。
